| E106b | Imperial forces raided the Waking Sands, slaughtered many Scions and left with prisoners. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "the Waking Sands was raided by imperial forces, who slaughtered countless Scions before departing─with prisoners." | #106 |
| E106c | The raiders came for the one who slew Ifrit and Titan. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "We have come for the one who slayed Ifrit and Titan!" | #106 |
| E106d | Minfilia offered to surrender if the innocents were spared. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "I surrender myself, on the condition you spare the innocents." | #106 |
| E106e | The dying sylph Noraxia sends you to the church in eastern Thanalan. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "At church in eastern Thanalan...walking one must claim sanctuary..." | #106 |
| E106f | Father Iliud has known Minfilia since childhood, swore to support her, and offers the same to her fellow Scions. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "I have known her since she was but a child... … I swore then to do everything in my power to support her, and I will gladly offer the selfsame service to her brother and sister Scions." | #106 |
| E106g | Marques, a survivor of Carteneau whom the church took in, is set to look after you. | *All Good Things* · 2.0 · main | "Marques is a survivor of the battle of Carteneau. … Had we not taken him into our care, he would surely have perished in the desert. … Marques, I want you to look after [Forename]." | #106 |
| E107a | Marques mended a broken horologe as though he had done it countless times. | *You Can't Take It with You* · 2.0 · main | "I knew exactly what to do, as though I had performed the same motions countless times before..." | #107 |
| E107b | Sister Eluned wonders whether the horologe's dead owner was a Garlean spy. | *You Can't Take It with You* · 2.0 · main | "You don't suppose that the owner might have been a Garlean spy?" | #107 |
| E108a | The fallen Scions are carried from the Waking Sands for their rites. | *Bringing out the Dead* · 2.0 · main | "the bodies of your comrades are presently being removed from the Waking Sands and prepared for transport here." | #108 |
| E108b | The church will do all it can to see the dead to Thal's realm. | *Bringing out the Dead* · 2.0 · main | "do everything we can to shepherd them to Thal's realm." | #108 |
| E109a | Noraxia's body is returned to her home in the Black Shroud. | *Bury Me Not on the Lone Prairie* · 2.0 · main | "Sister Eluned believes that it would be best if the [[Noraxia's Corpse|sylph's body]] was returned to her home in [[the Black Shroud]]" | #109 |
| E109b | You were with Noraxia at the end, and her people ask that you tell them she was brave. | *Bury Me Not on the Lone Prairie* · 2.0 · main | "Walking one was with Noraxia at the end... Tell this one that Noraxia was brave." | #109 |
| E109c | The sylph Komuxio demands vengeance on the imperials. | *Bury Me Not on the Lone Prairie* · 2.0 · main | "This one demands vengeance! Imperial ones must pay! Imperial ones must suffer!" | #109 |
| E109d | The remaining Scions are buried in the church's lichyard. | *Bury Me Not on the Lone Prairie* · 2.0 · main | "in your absence, we interred the remaining Scions in the lichyard." | #109 |
| E110a | A stranger attacks you in the lichyard, and you kill him in self-defense. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "you come under attack from an unknown assailant … After slaying the man in self-defense, you note that his sword is unlike any you have seen before" | #110 |
| E110b | Iliud knows the sword for Garlean steel: an imperial soldier was watching Marques. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "Recognizing the sword you found to be Garlean steel, Father Iliud concludes that the man observing Marques was an imperial soldier." | #110 |
| E110c | Alphinaud declares Marques to be Cid, missing since the Calamity five years ago. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "declares Marques to be none other than Master Cid … who has been missing since the Calamity five years ago." | #110 |
| E110d | Cid has not recovered his memories, but believes Alphinaud and joins him. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "While he has yet to recover his lost memories, Cid senses that the boy speaks the truth, and decides to join him." | #110 |
| E110e | The Ixal have summoned Garuda, and the surviving Scions need Cid's lost airship, the Enterprise, to reach her. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "that the Ixal have summoned Garuda, and that Cid's lost airship, the ''Enterprise'', will be required if the surviving Scions are to reach the primal." | #110 |
| E110f | The Enterprise was last seen over Gridania. | *Eyes on Me* · 2.0 · main | "Together with Cid and Alphinaud, make for Gridania, where the ''Enterprise'' was last seen." | #110 |
| E111a | A tale says the Enterprise flew northwest from Gridania toward Coerthas. | *He Who Waited Behind* · 2.0 · main | "It states that the ''Enterprise'' flew northwest from Gridania towards Coerthas." | #111 |
| E111b | Vortefaurt saw the airship pass. | *He Who Waited Behind* · 2.0 · main | "Vortefaurt confirms that he bore witness to the vessel's passing." | #111 |
| E112a | The Enterprise vanished over the mountains of Coerthas to the northwest. | *Cold Reception* · 2.0 · main | "According to Vortefaurt, the ''Enterprise'' disappeared over the mountains of [[Coerthas]] to the northwest." | #112 |
| E112b | Ser Ludovoix will not let an outsider read the Observatorium's records. | *Cold Reception* · 2.0 · main | "Hah, how absurd! As if we would permit an outsider to peruse the records of the Observatorium." | #112 |
| E112c | Ishgard lets foreigners travel but offers strangers no help. | *Cold Reception* · 2.0 · main | "The Holy See of Ishgard may allow foreigners to travel freely within these lands, but we do not intend to offer succor to strangers." | #112 |
| E113a | The knight's attackers were heretics sworn to the dragons. | *The Unending War* · 2.0 · main | "the [[Ishgard]]ian names the attackers as heretics─those who have pledged their allegiance to the [[dragon]]s." | #113 |
| E113b | The records bear on a war that has raged for generations. | *The Unending War* · 2.0 · main | "Were our records to fall into the wrong hands, it could change the course of a war which has raged for generations!" | #113 |
| E113c | Inquisitor Guillaime subtly warns you away from Ishgard's affairs. | *The Unending War* · 2.0 · main | "subtly cautioning you against becoming involved in Ishgardian affairs" | #113 |
| E114a | House Durendaire will still not open the Observatorium's records to you. | *Men of Honor* · 2.0 · main | "Even so, they will ''not'' grant you access to the Observatorium's records." | #114 |
| E114b | You must petition Lord Portelaine for introductions to the other High Houses. | *Men of Honor* · 2.0 · main | "Approach Lord [[Portelaine]] and petition him for formal introductions to the other [[High Houses]]" | #114 |
| E114c | Portelaine wants a third deed before he endorses you to three High Houses. | *Men of Honor* · 2.0 · main | "But while you have performed ''two'' acts, you would have me endorse you to ''three'' High Houses." | #114 |
| E115a | A draconian rosary turns up in a chest belonging to Lord Francel of House Haillenarte. | *Three for Three* · 2.0 · main | "a draconian rosary in a chest belonging to Lord [[Francel]] of [[House Haillenarte]]." | #115 |
| E115b | House Haillenarte is whispered to be full of heretics. | *Three for Three* · 2.0 · main | "House Haillenarte is whispered to be infested with heretics..." | #115 |
| E115c | Portelaine informs the inquisitors and warns you away from House Haillenarte. | *Three for Three* · 2.0 · main | "I shall inform the inquisitors immediately. Under these circumstances, I must strongly advise against associating with House Haillenarte." | #115 |
| E116a | Francel's former servant swears he is no heretic. | *The Rose and the Unicorn* · 2.0 · main | "Lord Francel is no heretic. He is a goodly, righteous man, wholly dedicated to the cause." | #116 |
| E116b | Francel fears the rosary will still be taken as proof against him. | *The Rose and the Unicorn* · 2.0 · main | "Although the draconian rosary was not found in my possession, it will still be considered by many to be proof of my" | #116 |
| E116c | Lord Haurchefant of House Fortemps commands the garrison at Camp Dragonhead. | *The Rose and the Unicorn* · 2.0 · main | "Lord Haurchefant of House Fortemps commands the garrison at Camp Dragonhead." | #116 |
| E116d | Francel gives you a letter of introduction to House Fortemps. | *The Rose and the Unicorn* · 2.0 · main | "he furnishes you with a [[Letter to Haurchefant|letter of introduction]] to [[House Fortemps]]" | #116 |
| E116e | Haurchefant promises to make inquiries for you. | *The Rose and the Unicorn* · 2.0 · main | "Nevertheless, I will make inquiries on your behalf and share with you my findings in the future." | #116 |
| E117a | Since Inquisitor Guillaime arrived, countless heretics have been found, even among Ishgard's elite. | *The Talk of Coerthas* · 2.0 · main | "Why, in the short time since Inquisitor Guillaime arrived in Coerthas, countless heretics have been discovered, even amongst the elite of Ishgardian society!" | #117 |
| E117b | The heresy accusations have thrown House Haillenarte into chaos. | *The Talk of Coerthas* · 2.0 · main | "But these accusations of heresy have thrown House Haillenarte into chaos." | #117 |
| E117c | All of Coerthas is consumed by talk of heretics in the High Houses. | *The Talk of Coerthas* · 2.0 · main | "It seems the whole of Coerthas is consumed by this talk of heretics in the High Houses." | #117 |
| E118a | Francel went to the Steel Vigil hoping to show his devotion by slaying scalekin. | *Road to Redemption* · 2.0 · main | "I had hoped we might demonstrate our devotion by slaying a number of the scalekin." | #118 |
| E118b | Francel's trial approaches. | *Road to Redemption* · 2.0 · main | "The day of my trial approaches." | #118 |
| E118c | A witness to the Enterprise's flight has been found, but is reluctant to meet you. | *Road to Redemption* · 2.0 · main | "the witness became understandably reticent to make your acquaintance" | #118 |
| E118d | Francel's name, and so yours, must be cleared first. | *Road to Redemption* · 2.0 · main | "we must first clear Lord Francel's name─and, by association, yours." | #118 |
| E119a | Haurchefant believes the rosary was planted. | *Following the Evidence* · 2.0 · main | "If the rosary was not his, then someone else with access ''must'' have planted it." | #119 |
| E119b | Two parcels bound for Skyfire Locks each held a draconian rosary. | *Following the Evidence* · 2.0 · main | "the two parcels bound for [[Skyfire Locks]] both contained a draconian rosary!" | #119 |
| E119c | Haurchefant calls it a crass attempt to sully House Haillenarte. | *Following the Evidence* · 2.0 · main | "Even the inquisitors must acknowledge that this is no more than a crass attempt to sully House Haillenarte's reputation." | #119 |
| E119d | Francel's trial is at hand. | *Following the Evidence* · 2.0 · main | "Lord Francel's trial is upon us, and we must act now if we are to clear his name!" | #119 |
| E120a | Inquisitor Guillaime presides over Francel's trial at Witchdrop. | *In the Eyes of Gods and Men* · 2.0 · main | "Inquisitor Guillaime presides over Lord Francel's trial at Witchdrop even as we speak." | #120 |
| E120b | Guillaime orders Francel to prove his innocence by leaping from the cliff. | *In the Eyes of Gods and Men* · 2.0 · main | "Leap from the cliff and entrust your fate to the Fury." | #120 |
| E120c | A heretic among the Temple Knights summoned the wyvern. | *In the Eyes of Gods and Men* · 2.0 · main | "it was ''he'' who summoned the wyvern!" | #120 |
| E120d | Guillaime withdraws the charges against Francel, pending investigation. | *In the Eyes of Gods and Men* · 2.0 · main | "I shall withdraw my charges against Lord Francel, pending further investigation." | #120 |
| E120e | Haurchefant says someone has used the trials to sow discord between the houses. | *In the Eyes of Gods and Men* · 2.0 · main | "They have used you and your fellows to sow discord, Inquisitor─to set brother against brother" | #120 |
| E121a | The Enterprise landed near the Stone Vigil days before the Calamity. | *The Final Flight of the Enterprise* · 2.0 · main | "he saw the ''Enterprise'' land near [[the Stone Vigil]] several days before [[the Calamity]]" | #121 |
| E121b | The Stone Vigil is now held by Dravanian forces. | *The Final Flight of the Enterprise* · 2.0 · main | "the Stone Vigil is currently held by [[Dravanian Horde|Dravanian forces]]" | #121 |
| E121c | Haurchefant writes to Lord Drillemont of Whitebrim Front to ask that you be let in. | *The Final Flight of the Enterprise* · 2.0 · main | "I will write a letter to Lord Drillemont of Whitebrim Front and request that he grant you permission to enter." | #121 |
| E121d | Those who tried to destroy House Haillenarte are still unmasked. | *The Final Flight of the Enterprise* · 2.0 · main | "the cravens who attempted to destroy House Haillenarte have yet to be unmasked" | #121 |
| E122a | Alphinaud tells Drillemont you came to slay Garuda and seek the Enterprise. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "We are come to Coerthas to slay Garuda, Lady of the Vortex. To that end, we seek the ''Enterprise''" | #122 |
| E122b | Drillemont is astonished that Cid is alive. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "you're ''alive''!? But that's impossible!" | #122 |
| E122c | Guillaime calls Cid presumed dead and the Scions a defunct band the Empire exterminated. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "missing and presumed dead since before the Calamity, and two Scions of the Seventh Dawn─a defunct band of misfits recently exterminated by the Empire." | #122 |
| E122d | Guillaime insists you must not be let through to the Stone Vigil. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "I know not why these three seek the Stone Vigil, but I know that we cannot permit them to pass." | #122 |
| E122e | Drillemont bars you until the outpost is retaken and you declare your true intentions. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "You three will not enter the outpost until we have reclaimed it─and until you declare your ''true'' intentions as well." | #122 |
| E122f | Alphinaud means to earn Drillemont's trust. | *Ye of Little Faith* · 2.0 · main | "we must do whatever it takes to earn Lord Drillemont's trust. Understood?" | #122 |
| E123a | The cook says winning over the barracks will catch Drillemont's notice. | *Factual Folklore* · 2.0 · main | "Win over the barracks, and he's sure to take notice." | #123 |
| E124a | Cid builds the infirmary a better alembic from its broken equipment. | *The Best Inventions* · 2.0 · main | "So I used some of their broken equipment to craft a more advanced alembic─both as a gesture of goodwill" | #124 |
| E124b | Working with his hands kindles familiar feelings in Cid. | *The Best Inventions* · 2.0 · main | "since working with my hands kindles familiar feelings." | #124 |
| E124c | Guillaime warns that your efforts serve an unknown end. | *The Best Inventions* · 2.0 · main | "Their earnest efforts are but a means to an as-yet-unknown end." | #124 |
| E124d | The inquisitor seems to hate you and your companions. | *The Best Inventions* · 2.0 · main | "The inquisitor appears to have developed a hatred for you and your associates" | #124 |
| E125a | The knight Joellaut says Guillaime carried him to the gates when he fell. | *Influencing Inquisitors* · 2.0 · main | "Inquisitor Guillaime rescued ''me'', carrying my unconscious body to the gates." | #125 |
| E125b | Alphinaud suspects Guillaime has other reasons for hindering you. | *Influencing Inquisitors* · 2.0 · main | "I am beginning to suspect he has other reasons for hindering us now." | #125 |
| E125c | Guillaime is credited with unmasking unprecedented numbers of heretics. | *Influencing Inquisitors* · 2.0 · main | "Under Inquisitor Guillaime's watchful gaze, unprecedented numbers of heretics have been unmasked and brought to justice." | #125 |
| E126a | A frozen corpse in the chasm closely resembles Guillaime. | *By the Lights of Ishgard* · 2.0 · main | "a frozen corpse in the chasm which bears a striking resemblance to Inquisitor Guillaime" | #126 |
| E126b | The real inquisitor was murdered on the day he arrived in Coerthas. | *By the Lights of Ishgard* · 2.0 · main | "Newly come to Coerthas and murdered on the day of his arrival..." | #126 |
| E126c | The corpse is the real Inquisitor Guillaime. | *By the Lights of Ishgard* · 2.0 · main | "I believe you have just made the acquaintance of the ''real'' Inquisitor Guillaime." | #126 |
| E126d | The impostor's identity and reasons for opposing you are unknown. | *By the Lights of Ishgard* · 2.0 · main | "we have yet to discern his true identity, or why he has decided to oppose us." | #126 |
| E127a | The impostor coerced Prunilla with threats against her loved ones. | *Blood for Blood* · 2.0 · main | "if I defied him...everyone I loved would suffer unimaginable horrors before they died." | #127 |
| E127b | The impostor condemned innocent people found carrying draconian rosaries. | *Blood for Blood* · 2.0 · main | "countless men and women of good moral standing, seized whilst in possession of draconian rosaries." | #127 |
| E127c | Prunilla planted rosaries from his chest in shipments bound for Skyfire Locks. | *Blood for Blood* · 2.0 · main | "I took several from his chest and planted them in shipments bound for Skyfire Locks." | #127 |
| E127d | Drillemont is convinced the inquisitor is a heretic. | *Blood for Blood* · 2.0 · main | "Fury take me, the inquisitor is a ''heretic''!?" | #127 |
| E127e | Drillemont will let you into the outpost, but first asks your help. | *Blood for Blood* · 2.0 · main | "I will permit you to enter the outpost─but first, I must needs beg your assistance." | #127 |
| E128a | Drillemont names the impostor a heretic and the murderer of Inquisitor Guillaime. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "I name thee ''heretic'', murderer of Inquisitor Guillaime and countless innocents!" | #128 |
| E128b | The impostor calls all Ishgard complicit in the archbishop's lies. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "''All'' are complicit in these crimes, for ''all'' live their lives by the archbishop's lies." | #128 |
| E128c | He summons his Dravanian allies and takes the form of a dragon. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "he summons his [[Dravanian Horde|Dravanian allies]] and endeavors to defend himself, going so far as to use his magicks to assume the form of a [[dragon]]." | #128 |
| E128d | He warns that the dragons in the Stone Vigil will tear you apart. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "The dragons within the Stone Vigil will rend you asunder." | #128 |
| E128e | Drillemont says it is time you were reunited with your airship. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "It is past time that you were reunited with your airship." | #128 |
| E128f | You defeat the false inquisitor. | *The Heretic among Us* · 2.0 · main | "Alphinaud refuses to revel in your victory over the false inquisitor" | #128 |
| E129a | Drillemont cannot say whether the Enterprise survived the outpost's fall. | *In Pursuit of the Past* · 2.0 · main | "Whether it was damaged or destroyed during the battle, I cannot say" | #129 |
| E129b | Lahabrea taunts that Garuda is stronger than any primal you have faced. | *In Pursuit of the Past* · 2.0 · main | "And you are aware that her strength greatly exceeds that of your previous primal conquests?" | #129 |
| E129c | Alphinaud and Cid slip aboard the Enterprise while you hold off the dragon. | *In Pursuit of the Past* · 2.0 · main | "Cid─you and I will sneak aboard the ''Enterprise'' and prepare it for launch." | #129 |
| E129d | Cid knows the airship, though he cannot say more. | *In Pursuit of the Past* · 2.0 · main | "I...I ''know'' this airship." | #129 |
| E129e | The Enterprise is in no condition to weather Garuda's storm. | *In Pursuit of the Past* · 2.0 · main | "it is plain the ''Enterprise'' is in no condition to weather the storm" | #129 |
| E130a | The battered Enterprise cannot survive the winds around the Howling Eye, Garuda's sanctuary. | *Into the Eye of the Storm* · 2.0 · main | "In its current condition, the ''Enterprise'' cannot endure the tumultuous winds which encircle [[the Howling Eye]], [[Garuda]]'s sanctuary." | #130 |
| E130b | Cid would use a corrupted crystal to change the aspect of Garuda's wind barrier. | *Into the Eye of the Storm* · 2.0 · main | "we could theoretically use a corrupted crystal to convert the wind-aspected aether comprising Garuda's barrier into aether of another aspect" | #130 |
| E130c | Cid knows of a scholar, Lamberteint, in Camp Drybone. | *Into the Eye of the Storm* · 2.0 · main | "A scholar by the name of Lamberteint, who resides in Camp Drybone." | #130 |
| E130d | Cid met Lamberteint while he lived as Marques at the church. | *Into the Eye of the Storm* · 2.0 · main | "Strange as it may sound, I met him during my time at the church. Well, ''Marques'' met him, I suppose." | #130 |
| E130e | The Seedseers house the Enterprise for its repairs. | *Into the Eye of the Storm* · 2.0 · main | "The Seedseers have granted our request to house the ''Enterprise''." | #130 |
| E131a | Lamberteint lends you a warded pot and sends you to his pupil Hahasako at Highbridge. | *Sealed with Science* · 2.0 · main | "take the pot to Hahasako, a student of mine monitoring the flow of aether at Highbridge." | #131 |
| E132a | The crystal from the Burning Wall is wind-aspected and would only strengthen the barrier. | *With the Utmost Care* · 2.0 · main | "A corrupted crystal overflowing with wind-aspected aether. … this crystal would only serve to ''strengthen'' the barrier." | #132 |
| E133a | Another pupil, Ceana, has found a promising site at Pharos Sirius on the Isles of Umbra. | *A Promising Prospect* · 2.0 · main | "It was ''western'' La Noscea─Pharos Sirius, on the Isles of Umbra, to be precise." | #133 |
| E134a | Access to the isles is prohibited. | *It's Probably Not Pirates* · 2.0 · main | "all access to the isles is prohibited at present" | #134 |
| E134b | The Yellowjackets speak of spirits and undead on the isles. | *It's Probably Not Pirates* · 2.0 · main | "The three Yellowjackets you elect to question speak of spirits and undead infesting the Isles of Umbra." | #134 |
| E135a | Mimidoa gives you a writ of passage if you look into the rumors for him. | *Representing the Representative* · 2.0 · main | "Mimidoa offers you a [[Permission for Passage|writ of passage]], on the condition that you look into the rumors on his behalf." | #135 |
| E135b | Skyfryn, back from the isles, says a woman calls and sings to him. | *Representing the Representative* · 2.0 · main | "I...I have to go back. She calls to me...''sings'' to me..." | #135 |
| E136a | Ceana flees back to the mainland in fear. | *The Reluctant Researcher* · 2.0 · main | "In the end, Ceana succumbs to her fear and flees back to the mainland" | #136 |
| E136b | The gate guard, Davyd, cannot give you a crystal. | *The Reluctant Researcher* · 2.0 · main | "Though [[Davyd]] understands your intent, he is nevertheless unable to oblige you." | #136 |
| E137a | Davyd will bring you a crystal if you help solve the mystery. | *Sweet Somethings* · 2.0 · main | "help me unravel this mystery, and I'll bring you your corrupted crystal." | #137 |
| E137b | Adventurers speak of an irresistible voice at the Ship Graveyard. | *Sweet Somethings* · 2.0 · main | "The adventurers speak of a beautiful, irresistible voice heard at [[the Ship Graveyard]], which may be connected to the undead." | #137 |
| E138a | Mimidoa is sure a siren is behind the trouble. | *History Repeating* · 2.0 · main | "Aye...I'd bet me bum ear a siren's behind all this." | #138 |
| E138b | The siren retreats into the depths. | *History Repeating* · 2.0 · main | "before at last relenting and retreating to the depths whence she came." | #138 |
| E138c | Davyd gives you the corrupted crystal as promised. | *History Repeating* · 2.0 · main | "the corrupted crystal you wanted, safely sealed within the pot, just as you requested." | #138 |
| E138d | The crystal is fire-aspected, so it won't help against the wind. | *History Repeating* · 2.0 · main | "the corrupted shard is the manifestation of an over-abundance of the element of fire" | #138 |
| E139a | A spriggan has devoured every corrupted crystal in the Twelveswood. | *The Curious Case of Giggity* · 2.0 · main | "one such creature with an infamously insatiable appetite has single-handedly devoured every corrupted crystal in the Twelveswood." | #139 |
| E139b | You cut the crystal from the spriggan's gut. | *The Curious Case of Giggity* · 2.0 · main | "you rip and tear open the spriggan's huge guts and claim the corrupted crystal nestled deep within." | #139 |
| E139c | Hedyn confirms the crystal is ice-aspected. | *The Curious Case of Giggity* · 2.0 · main | "Hedyn confirms that the foul-smelling, offal-laden crystal is comprised of ice-aspected aether." | #139 |
| E140a | Cid's work on the Enterprise is nearly done; only the crystal remains to be tested. | *Better Late than Never* · 2.0 · main | "Cid's work on the ''Enterprise'' is nearly complete, and all that remains is for him to test the crystal you have brought." | #140 |
| E140b | Cid says nothing will stand between you and Garuda. | *Better Late than Never* · 2.0 · main | "Once you bring me that crystal, nothing will stand between us and Garuda." | #140 |
| E140c | Cid says this is what he was born to do. | *Better Late than Never* · 2.0 · main | "This...this is me. This is what I was born to do." | #140 |
| E141a | The Enterprise is ready to fly to the Howling Eye. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "the ''Enterprise'' is finally ready to make the journey to [[the Howling Eye]]." | #141 |
| E141b | With the Echo you guide Cid through his lost memories of leaving Garlemald for Eorzea. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "you guide him through a series of forgotten memories, detailing the path that led the once-loyal engineer to turn his back on [[Garlemald]] and flee to [[Eorzea]]." | #141 |
| E141c | You strike Garuda down, but her Ixali worshippers' belief raises her again. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "only to see her rise once more, reinvigorated by the belief of her [[Ixal]]i disciples." | #141 |
| E141d | Garuda's dying kobold and Amalj'aa captives cry out and summon their own gods. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "slaughtering her captive [[kobold]]s and [[Amalj'aa]], whose cries of agony serve to summon their own patron deities." | #141 |
| E141e | All efforts now turn to destroying the Empire's weapon. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "all efforts must now be focused upon destroying [[Garlean Empire|the Empire]]'s [[The Ultima Weapon|ultimate weapon]]." | #141 |
| E141f | Cid and Alphinaud resolve to re-form the Scions at the Waking Sands. | *Lady of the Vortex* · 2.0 · main | "Believing the best course of action is to reform [[the Scions of the Seventh Dawn]], they decide to return to [[the Waking Sands]]." | #141 |
| E142a | Alphinaud brings you back to the Waking Sands. | *Reclamation* · 2.0 · main | "It is past time that we return to the Waking Sands." | #142 |
| E142b | Yda learns that Papalymo and Minfilia were not among the dead. | *Reclamation* · 2.0 · main | "Papalymo and Minfilia weren't among the dead, according to the church." | #142 |
| E142c | Alphinaud believes Gaius means to use Minfilia to understand the Echo. | *Reclamation* · 2.0 · main | "He means to use Minfilia to gain an understanding of the Echo." | #142 |
| E142d | The captured Scions are held at Castrum Centri, an imperial stronghold in Mor Dhona. | *Reclamation* · 2.0 · main | "your comrades are being held at [[Castrum Centri]], an imperial stronghold in [[Mor Dhona]]." | #142 |
| E143a | Y'shtola wants intelligence first, from Lord Portelaine at the Observatorium. | *Casing the Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "she suggests traveling to [[the Observatorium]] in [[Coerthas]], and there petitioning Lord [[Portelaine]] for aid." | #143 |
| E143b | Four prisoners matching Minfilia, Urianger, Papalymo and Tataru were seen led into Castrum Centri. | *Casing the Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "matching the descriptions of [[Minfilia Warde|Minfilia]], [[Urianger]], [[Papalymo]], and [[Tataru]] were observed being led into Castrum Centri." | #143 |
| E143c | Two men, likely Biggs and Wedge, escaped a downed imperial airship. | *Casing the Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "two men─likely [[Biggs]] and [[Wedge]]─escaped from a downed imperial airship not long ago." | #143 |
| E143d | Y'shtola reasons the captives will not be executed until the study is complete. | *Casing the Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "they will not be executed until such time as the study is complete." | #143 |
| E143e | Gaius sends Rhitahtyn to Castrum Occidens. | *Casing the Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "Rhitahtyn. You depart for Castrum Occidens at midnight." | #143 |
| E144a | Imperial soldiers are hunting the escapees. | *Eyes on the Empire* · 2.0 · main | "Imperial soldiers have since been sighted in the area, seeking to recapture them." | #144 |
| E145a | Wedge says Biggs went another way to lure their pursuers off. | *Footprints in the Snow* · 2.0 · main | "He went off in a different direction to lure our pursuers away." | #145 |
| E145b | Biggs headed west. | *Footprints in the Snow* · 2.0 · main | "At any rate, we know for a fact that Biggs headed west." | #145 |
| E145c | Wedge recognizes Cid, alive after five years. | *Footprints in the Snow* · 2.0 · main | "Dare I hope...? Was that...Master" | #145 |
| E145d | Cid says Biggs has always taken care of Wedge. | *Footprints in the Snow* · 2.0 · main | "For all the tongue-lashings he gives Wedge, he's always taken care of him." | #145 |
| E146a | The Fury's Gaze holds only vengeful ghosts. | *Monumental Hopes* · 2.0 · main | "Your search of the Fury's Gaze yields nothing but a confrontation with vengeful ghosts." | #146 |
| E146b | Wedge is still determined to find Biggs. | *Monumental Hopes* · 2.0 · main | "is no less determined to find his companion." | #146 |
| E147a | With Yda and Y'shtola you rescue Biggs from imperial troops. | *Notorious Biggs* · 2.0 · main | "With the aid of [[Yda]] and [[Y'shtola]], you succeed in rescuing Biggs from a detachment of imperial troops." | #147 |
| E147b | Y'shtola believes the raiders arrived by teleportation of the kind the Ascians use. | *Notorious Biggs* · 2.0 · main | "they were borne there by potent teleportation magicks─magicks of the kind used by the Ascians." | #147 |
| E147c | Biggs and Wedge were held at Castrum Centri apart from Minfilia and the others. | *Notorious Biggs* · 2.0 · main | "we found ourselves in the midst of an imperial stronghold─Castrum Centri. … Biggs and I were thrown into a detainment cell together, while Minfilia and the others were taken elsewhere." | #147 |
| E147d | They escaped by sabotaging an airship's altitude control. | *Notorious Biggs* · 2.0 · main | "we sabotaged the ship's altitude control and thereby forced an emergency landing." | #147 |
| E147e | Cid and his two core Ironworks members are together again. | *Notorious Biggs* · 2.0 · main | "the three core members of [[Garlond Ironworks]] are together again" | #147 |
| E148a | Cid means to march in disguised as imperial troops. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "Aye, we'll disguise ourselves as imperial troops and march right in." | #148 |
| E148b | Castrum Centri is on high alert. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "Not even a fly could get within a hundred yalms of the walls undetected." | #148 |
| E148c | Glaumunt has a grudge against the Empire and knows Castrum Centri. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "The fellow's got a personal grudge against the Empire, and happens to know a deal about Castrum Centri besides." | #148 |
| E148d | Y'shtola and Yda go to scout the stronghold. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "Yda and I shall go to reconnoiter Garlean activity at the stronghold." | #148 |
| E148e | Glaumunt will help you. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "If his response is any indication, it would seem you can indeed expect his aid." | #148 |
| L148a | Cid sends you to Revenant's Toll in Mor Dhona to gather information on the captive Scions. | *Come-Into-My-Castrum* · 2.0 · main | "Cid bids you make your way to [[Revenant's Toll]] in [[Mor Dhona]], and solicit the local [[adventurer]]s for information." | #148 |
| E149a | A guard wonders why the tribunus is so obsessed with Minfilia. | *Getting Even with Garlemald* · 2.0 · main | "I do wonder, why is the tribunus so obsessed with this Minfilia woman?" | #149 |
| E149b | The guards say Minfilia has a mystical power the Garleans lack. | *Getting Even with Garlemald* · 2.0 · main | "They say she possesses some mystical power. Something we Garleans don't have." | #149 |
| E149c | Alphinaud is sure the captives are inside Castrum Centri. | *Getting Even with Garlemald* · 2.0 · main | "So Minfilia and the others are indeed at Castrum Centri!" | #149 |
| E149d | Glaumunt is seeking a suit of magitek armor. | *Getting Even with Garlemald* · 2.0 · main | "Glaumunt is looking into ways to procure a suit of magitek armor." | #149 |
| E149e | Glaumunt shows you a drainage pipe from the command tower for listening in. | *Getting Even with Garlemald* · 2.0 · main | "He tells you of a drainage pipe that leads from the stronghold's command tower" | #149 |
| E150a | Cid prepares to jam imperial communications using crystals that amplify their energy, though he is reluctant to use them. | *Drowning Out the Voices* · 2.0 · main | "Cid is satisfied that the crystals will suit his needs, though he expresses his reluctance to harness their power." | #150 |
| E150c | Glaumunt's plan is to send you into Castrum Centri in imperial dress. | *Acting the Part* · 2.0 · main | "The plan's to have you enter Castrum Centri all decked out in imperial duds" | #150b |
| E150d | You learn the imperial salute by watching patrols. | *Acting the Part* · 2.0 · main | "You have committed the imperial salute to your memory." | #150b |
| E151a | Three sets of imperial gear are needed: for Biggs, Wedge and you. | *Dressed for Conquest* · 2.0 · main | "three sets of gear, one for each member of the infiltration team─Biggs, Wedge, and yourself." | #151 |
| E151b | Sark Malark, a friend of Glaumunt's, helps with the disguises, and Eginolf repairs the gear. | *Dressed for Conquest* · 2.0 · main | "I have heard of your plight from my friend Glaumunt. … Yes, these will serve beautifully. The great Eginolf never fails to impress." | #151 |
| E153a | Glaumunt fed the imperials false intelligence to draw out a patrol. | *Fool Me Twice (Quest)* · 2.0 · main | "I took the liberty o' feedin' 'em a bit o' the old false intelligence" | #153 |
| E153b | You seize a magitek reaper, which needs repairs. | *Fool Me Twice (Quest)* · 2.0 · main | "You have succeeded in acquiring a magitek reaper, though some repairs will be required before it can be put to use." | #153 |
| E153c | Biggs and Wedge repair the reaper at Cid's workshop. | *Fool Me Twice (Quest)* · 2.0 · main | "have already set about repairing the magitek reaper." | #153 |
| E155a | Y'shtola and Yda confront Livia, who led the raid on the Waking Sands. | *Escape from Castrum Centri* · 2.0 · main | "'Twas you who led the raid on the Waking Sands─you who butchered unarmed men and women for the crime of being unworthy of capture." | #155 |
| E155b | Y'shtola and Yda rode into the castrum aboard your vehicle. | *Escape from Castrum Centri* · 2.0 · main | "There chanced to be a commotion outside the castrum's walls which allowed us to avail ourselves of the comforts of your vehicle." | #155 |
| M162a | Minfilia holds that the Scions' neutrality is fundamental to their cause. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "Our neutrality is fundamental to our cause." | #162 |
| M162b | Since the Scions defeated Gaius and destroyed his weapon, the whole world watches them. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "Ever since we defeated Gaius van Baelsar and destroyed his ultimate weapon, the eyes of the world have followed our every move" | #162 |
| M162c | Y'shtola warns that the Scions' dealings with the city-states have already weakened their claim to neutrality. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "Our many dalliances with the city–states have already weakened our claim to neutrality" | #162 |
| M162d | Urianger reports that the beast tribes are quietly working to raise their fallen primals again. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "the beast tribes do labor unseen, defiant in defeat, to raise up their fallen primals once more." | #162 |
| M162e | Alphinaud tells Minfilia the Scions must leave Ul'dah. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "Minfilia─the Scions of the Seventh Dawn must leave Ul'dah." | #162 |
| M162f | Alphinaud proposes moving the Scions' headquarters to Mor Dhona, beyond the great nations' borders. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "Alphinaud seizes the opportunity to make a bold counter-proposal: move the Scions' headquarters to [[Mor Dhona]], beyond the borders of [[Ul'dah]] and the other great nations." | #162 |
| M162g | Alphinaud says the Syndicate will not let the Scions stay independent, and is why Vesper Bay still lacks an aetheryte. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "We have demonstrated our capabilities, and the Syndicate has taken note. They will not suffer our organization to remain independent now. … Surely you realize they are the reason Vesper Bay still lacks an aetheryte?" | #162 |
| M162h | Minfilia calls Alphinaud's idea reckless and dismisses him. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "decrying his idea as reckless beyond words before unceremoniously dismissing him." | #162 |
| M162i | Refugees hope the Scions will help liberate Ala Mhigo. | *The Price of Principles* · 2.1 · main | "there is a growing belief amongst the refugees that Ala Mhigo could be liberated...if only the Scions would commit their strength to the cause." | #162 |
| M163a | Alphinaud believes Minfilia won't leave Ul'dah because she hopes her late mother, F'lhaminn, still lives. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "the Antecedent's blind refusal to contemplate leaving [[Ul'dah]] stems from the empty hope that her late mother [[F'lhaminn]] still lives." | #163 |
| M163b | Minfilia's adoptive mother, F'lhaminn, the Songstress of Ul'dah, was believed to have died in the Calamity. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "Minfilia's mother─well, adoptive mother─was among the great many who perished during the Calamity. … F'lhaminn was her name, though you may know her better as the “Songstress of Ul'dah.”" | #163 |
| M163c | Rumors say the songstress survived the Calamity. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "For whatever reason, rumors have recently begun to circulate that the famed songstress did not perish in the fires of [[the Calamity]]." | #163 |
| M163d | F'lhaminn means to reveal herself to Minfilia when the time is right. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "You will be pleased to know that I fully intend to reveal myself to Minfilia─when the time is right." | #163 |
| M163e | F'lhaminn warns that not everyone who seeks her means well. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "Not everyone who endeavors to find me does so with the best intentions, child." | #163 |
| M163f | The stage is set for Minfilia and F'lhaminn's reunion. | *Moving On* · 2.1 · main | "Thanks to your efforts, the stage is now set for a long-overdue reunion between Minfilia and F'lhaminn." | #163 |
| M164a | Minfilia's father brought her to Ul'dah as a child and died in an accident soon after. | *All Things in Time* · 2.1 · main | "When I was yet a child, he brought me here to Ul'dah. The accident which claimed his life happened shortly after our arrival." | #164 |
| M164b | F'lhaminn stayed hidden because the imperials came for her. | *All Things in Time* · 2.1 · main | "The imperials came for me, as I always knew they would." | #164 |
| M164c | F'lhaminn tells Minfilia that she and the Scions must leave Ul'dah. | *All Things in Time* · 2.1 · main | "You and the Scions ''must'' leave Ul'dah." | #164 |
| M164d | Minfilia agrees to a new headquarters in Revenant's Toll. | *All Things in Time* · 2.1 · main | "We shall establish a new headquarters in Revenant's Toll, as you proposed." | #164 |
| M164e | F'lhaminn kept the cat's-eye Minfilia found as a child. | *All Things in Time* · 2.1 · main | "This...this is the cat's-eye I found! All these years you kept it..." | #164 |
| M165a | The Adventurers' Guild gives the Scions facilities in exchange for helping defend and develop Revenant's Toll. | *Laying the Foundation* · 2.1 · main | "Our friends at the Adventurers' Guild have agreed to furnish us with new facilities and material support in exchange for our assistance in the ongoing defense and development of Revenant's Toll." | #165 |
| M165b | The Scions' new home in Mor Dhona is the Rising Stones. | *Laying the Foundation* · 2.1 · main | "The Scions of the Seventh Dawn shall have a new home in Mor Dhona: the Rising Stones." | #165 |
| M165c | The Alliance leaders have each pledged their full support to the move. | *Laying the Foundation* · 2.1 · main | "I personally informed the Alliance leaders of our plans, and they have each pledged their full support─General Raubahn included." | #165 |
| M165d | Minfilia decides the Scions will cast off their secrecy and announce themselves to the world. | *Laying the Foundation* · 2.1 · main | "Moreover, I have decided that the time is right for us to cast off the vestiges of our order's veil of secrecy and announce ourselves to the world." | #165 |
| M165e | Minfilia sends you with documents to Slafborn. | *Laying the Foundation* · 2.1 · main | "I would like you to deliver these documents to Slafborn." | #165 |
| M166a | Another primal, or something like one, may have been summoned in Gridania. | *It's Possibly a Primal* · 2.1 · main | "We have reason to believe that another primal─or an entity resembling one─has been summoned in Gridania." | #166 |
| M166b | Urianger calls it a familiar foe, unlike Garuda. | *It's Possibly a Primal* · 2.1 · main | "'tis a familiar foe we face, though one quite unlike the Lady of the Vortex..." | #166 |
| M166c | The readings resemble ones taken more than five years ago. | *It's Possibly a Primal* · 2.1 · main | "the readings bear a strong resemblance to ones observed more than five years ago." | #166 |
| M166d | The Twin Adder's commander asks for you by name. | *It's Possibly a Primal* · 2.1 · main | "[[Vorsaile Heuloix|High Serpent Commander Heuloix]] of [[the Order of the Twin Adder]] has requested you by name, and Minfilia bids you answer his summons." | #166 |
| M167a | Kuplo Kopp warned of a threat to all mooglekind and the Twelveswood. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "He spoke of an imminent threat to all mooglekind, one which would imperil the entire Twelveswood if left unchecked." | #167 |
| M167b | Good King Moggle Mog XII has returned to Gridania after five years. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "has returned to Gridania after a five-year absence." | #167 |
| M167c | The Elder Seedseer believes the king is a myth made manifest, much as beast tribes summon their gods. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "It is our belief that Good King Moggle Mog XII is a myth made manifest via means akin to those employed by the beast tribes in the summoning of their gods." | #167 |
| M167d | The Elder Seedseer asks you to confront the king and drive him from the forest. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "Thus do we beseech you, [Forename]─confront Good King Moggle Mog XII and drive him from our midst." | #167 |
| M167e | The Mogglesguard and their liege lord hide behind magical wards. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "The sanctuary of the Mogglesguard and their liege lord is concealed by magical wards." | #167 |
| M167f | Papalymo fears the king sways the moogles as a primal would. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "We cannot discount the possibility that this...''entity'' is influencing the moogles in a manner similar to that of a primal." | #167 |
| M167g | Kuplo Kopp says the Mogglesguard only want to protect the forest from outsiders. | *Hail to the King, Kupo* · 2.1 · main | "the Mogglesguard only want to protect the forest from outsiders." | #167 |
| M168a | The Mogglesguard entrusted Kuplo Kopp with the keystones to the king's wards. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "Kuplo Kopp confided in me that he had been entrusted with a set of keystones by the Mogglesguard themselves." | #168 |
| M168b | When the king was first summoned five years ago, the keystones' guardians had to be slain. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "It was with great regret that we were forced to sanction the slaying of the guardians who then held the keystones" | #168 |
| M168c | With a heavy heart, Kuplo Kopp agrees to help you. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "With a heavy heart, Kuplo Kopp consents to your plan, and bids you take the ferry to [[Sweetbloom Pier]]." | #168 |
| M168d | The Mogglesguard threaten Kuplo Kopp for defying the king. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "Kuplo Kopp will answer for his crimes soon enough, as will all who defy the will of Good King Moggle Mog XII─may he reign forevermore, kupo!" | #168 |
| M168e | The king is slain and disintegrates into aether, leaving no corpse. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "The loyal Mogglesguard can only watch in horror as their beloved king of legend disintegrates into aether, leaving behind no corpse." | #168 |
| M168f | All mooglekind owes you a debt. | *You Have Selected Regicide* · 2.1 · main | "All mooglekind owes you a debt, kupo!" | #168 |
| M169a | The king may not be a primal by the accepted definition, but he manifested as one. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "According to the accepted definition, he may not be a primal...but the mode of his manifestation was in every respect the same." | #169 |
| M169b | Papalymo says the business reeks of the Ascians. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "Ascians. This whole business ''reeks'' of their handiwork!" | #169 |
| M169c | A masked man taught the Mogglesguard the summoning rite years ago. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "they said it was a masked man who had taught them how, years ago─b-but Gridania has no shortage of masked men" | #169 |
| M169d | The king was called forth by a rite the Ascians gave the Mogglesguard. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "was called forth by means of a rite imparted to the Mogglesguard by the [[Ascian]]s." | #169 |
| M169e | The Elder Seedseer will send more supplies to Revenant's Toll. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "We shall be glad to send additional supplies to Revenant's Toll." | #169 |
| M169f | Minfilia concludes that Ascians were behind both of the king's appearances. | *On the Properties of Primals* · 2.1 · main | "she too is forced to conclude that Ascians were behind both of Good King Moggle Mog XII's recent appearances." | #169 |
| M170a | The Scions leave for Mor Dhona in small groups. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "Rather than leaving en masse, [[the Scions]] will travel to [[Mor Dhona]] in several small groups." | #170 |
| M170b | Urianger will not join the Scions at Revenant's Toll. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "the Antecedent explains that [[Urianger]] will not be joining you at [[Revenant's Toll]]" | #170 |
| M170c | A white-robed Ascian slipped into the Scions' inner sanctum just before Minfilia collapsed. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "an [[Ascian]] robed in white was present mere moments before, having infiltrated the Scions' inner sanctum unnoticed." | #170 |
| M170d | He calls himself an emissary, and says mastering the Echo could end the strife between the Ascians and the children of Hydaelyn. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "the trespasser proclaims himself an emissary, and speaks cryptically of the Echo and its power, asserting that mastery of this gift could end hostilities between the [[Ascians]] and the children of [[Hydaelyn]] forever." | #170 |
| M170e | He knows you as the one who bested Lahabrea in the Praetorium. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "Although he recognizes you as the one who bested [[Lahabrea]] in [[the Praetorium]]," | #170 |
| M170f | He claims to serve the same god as Lahabrea. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "the Ascian emissary claims to serve [[Zodiark|the same god]] as Lahabrea" | #170 |
| M170g | He says Hydaelyn's influence is waning. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "Her influence wanes, and Her strength shall soon be spent." | #170 |
| M170h | Minfilia finds that Elidibus's words reveal little beyond his disdain for Lahabrea. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "The words of this Elidibus portend much but reveal nothing─save perhaps a measure of disdain for Lahabrea." | #170 |
| M170i | Minfilia has Urianger ask the Students of Baldesion to search their archives. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "Urianger, send word to the Students of Baldesion. Tell them to scour the archives─the forbidden tomes in particular." | #170 |
| M170j | Elidibus says that, unlike the Ascian before him, he has no quarrel with Minfilia. | *The Gifted* · 2.1 · main | "Mine are the robes of an emissary. Unlike he who came before, I have no quarrel with you." | #170 |
| M171a | Minfilia's father, Warburton, was a Garlean spy loyal to the Ala Mhigan Resistance. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "Minfilia's father, Warburton, was a [[Garlean]] spy whose true loyalties lay with the [[Ala Mhigan Resistance]]." | #171 |
| M171b | His journal holds everything his agents could steal about the primals. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "It contains every shred of information he and his agents could steal on what they believed to be the single greatest threat to Eorzea: the primals." | #171 |
| M171c | Slafborn hands the Rising Stones over to the Scions. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "The Rising Stones is officially yours!" | #171 |
| M171d | The Scions declare that they serve all of Eorzea openly and are beholden to no nation. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "We shall henceforth be beholden to no nation, but serve all of Eorzea's people, proudly and openly." | #171 |
| M171e | The Students of Baldesion have not answered Urianger. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "Mine every attempt to contact the Students of Baldesion hath been met with silence." | #171 |
| M171f | Minfilia believes Lahabrea endures and may return. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "But I was wrong. He endures, and may yet return." | #171 |
| M171g | Elidibus tells another Ascian that the Scions survived the Seventh Ardor and are stronger in the gift. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "They survived the Seventh Ardor and are stronger now in the gift." | #171 |
| M171h | Another Ascian says their plans are in motion and Elidibus's intentions unclear. | *Build on the Stone* · 2.1 · main | "We question. Our plans are in motion, your intentions unclear." | #171 |
| M172a | The crystal stores of a mining concern in Horizon have been secretly pillaged. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "During a recent audit, it was discovered that the crystal stores of [[Amajina & Sons Mineral Concern]] have been covertly pillaged." | #172 |
| M172b | Minfilia fears the thief may serve a primal. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "If there is even a possibility that the thief acts not out of self-interest but in service to a primal, we can ill afford to wait for the Blades to realize their mistake." | #172 |
| M172c | The suspects caught fleeing were only somnus smugglers. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "the fugitives were no more than common somnus smugglers" | #172 |
| M172d | Nothing suggests the Amalj'aa have the crystals. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "According to Thancred, there is nothing to suggest that the Amalj'aa have come into possession of significant quantities of crystals." | #172 |
| M172e | Thancred is sure these are no simple thieves. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "It cannot be that we are dealing with simple thieves." | #172 |
| M172f | Travelers from Doma seek an audience with the ruler of these lands. | *Still Waters* · 2.2 · main | "Far across the sea, from the land of Doma, have we traveled. We seek audience with the ruler of these lands." | #172 |
| M173a | Y'shtola poses as a crystal courier to bait the thieves. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "Posing as a courier in possession of crystals, Y'shtola will act as bait, while you and Thancred observe from a distance." | #173 |
| M173b | The thieves will not be taken alive. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "but when the trap is sprung, it quickly becomes clear that they will not be taken alive." | #173 |
| M173c | All the thieves were Sea Wolves with blue facial tattoos. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "Thancred notes that all were [[Sea Wolves]], and that each of them sported distinctive blue facial tattoos." | #173 |
| M173d | A traitor in the Brass Blades, a Lominsan-born Sea Wolf, escaped arrest. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "the traitorous [[Brass Blade]] was identified while you were away, but that the [[Limsa Lominsa|Lominsan]]-born [[Sea Wolf]] subdued the unit sent to detain him and took flight." | #173 |
| M173e | Thancred is placed in charge of matters in Thanalan. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "Having been placed in charge of matters in [[Thanalan]], Thancred invites you to return with him to [[Horizon]] and report to Fufulupa." | #173 |
| M173f | Y'shtola notes the thieves are a long way from home. | *A Final Temptation* · 2.2 · main | "Our thieves are a long way from home." | #173 |
| M174a | Travelers from Doma have come to Vesper Bay seeking Ul'dah's rulers. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "Alphinaud informs you that travelers from the eastern nation of [[Doma]] arrived in Vesper Bay not long ago, apparently seeking an audience with the rulers of [[Ul'dah]]." | #174 |
| M174b | Doma rose against the Empire when Garlemald's war of succession broke out. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "When the war of succession broke out in Garlemald, we espied an opportunity to free ourselves from the yoke of imperial oppression, and took up arms." | #174 |
| M174c | Doma was razed as an example to the other provinces. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "Doma is gone. Razed to the ground as an example to the other provinces." | #174 |
| M174d | Lolorito, the Syndicate's most influential member, persuades the others to refuse the Domans asylum. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "the most influential member of the Syndicate, [[Lolorito]], persuades the others to refuse the Domans asylum." | #174 |
| M174e | Lolorito claims refugees are prone to violence and crime. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "And need I remind you that these refugees are prone to violence and criminal activity?" | #174 |
| M174f | The Domans are granted an audience with the sultana and the Syndicate. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "the Domans have been granted an audience with [[Nanamo Ul Namo|the sultana]] and [[the Syndicate]]." | #174 |
| M174g | The sultana, Raubahn and Teledji sympathize with Yugiri's cause. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "Although the sultana, Raubahn, and [[Teledji Adeledji]] are sympathetic to her cause, the most influential member of the Syndicate" | #174 |
| M174h | Raubahn says that with the Empire no longer an immediate threat, the nations see little reason to stay united. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "Now that the Empire no longer poses an immediate threat, they see little reason to maintain the pretense of unity." | #174 |
| M174i | Yugiri's people wait on a ship in Vesper Bay under borrowed colors. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "On a ship anchored in Vesper Bay, flying borrowed colors." | #174 |
| M174j | Barred from Ul'dah, Yugiri and her people must find a new home. | *The Mother of Exiles* · 2.2 · main | "Thus barred from Ul'dah, Yugiri and her people must look elsewhere for a new home." | #174 |
| M175a | The Syndicate's vote stands. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "The Syndicate has spoken, and I see no point in moving that the matter be reconsidered." | #175 |
| M175b | The Domans could work as frontier hands at Revenant's Toll for food and shelter. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "Rather than petitioning another nation for aid, the Domans might instead travel to the outpost of [[Revenant's Toll]], where they could work as frontier hands in exchange for food and shelter." | #175 |
| M175c | The Scions' headquarters stands in Revenant's Toll, an outpost in Mor Dhona. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "The headquarters of my order, the Scions of the Seventh Dawn, stands in a place called Revenant's Toll, an outpost in Mor Dhona." | #175 |
| M175d | Teledji hires the Seventy-Seven Caravans for the Domans. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "I will engage the services of the Seventy-Seven Caravans on the Domans' behalf." | #175 |
| M175e | Raubahn, as general of the Immortal Flames, says escorting the Domans exceeds his mandate. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "Transportation remains our greatest obstacle, General. Is there aught the Immortal Flames can do? … I fear that exceeds our mandate." | #175 |
| M175f | Raubahn offers to discuss what aid the Immortal Flames can give the Domans. | *Promises to Keep* · 2.2 · main | "Lady Yugiri, if you and your people would accompany me, we may discuss what aid the Immortal Flames can provide" | #175 |
| M176a | The Domans prepare to leave Vesper Bay. | *Yugiri's Game* · 2.2 · main | "As we speak, the Domans prepare for departure at Vesper Bay, wherefrom they will be transported by carriage to Ul'dah." | #176 |
| M176b | Yugiri taught the children a hiding game in case the Garleans found them. | *Yugiri's Game* · 2.2 · main | "Lady Yugiri thought it best that the children know what to do in case the Garleans found us." | #176 |
| M176c | Three children are still hiding. | *Yugiri's Game* · 2.2 · main | "The only ones who have yet to be located are a trio who were engaged in a game of hide-and-seek." | #176 |
| M177a | You make the road to Ul'dah safe for the Domans' carriage. | *Why We Adventure* · 2.2 · main | "Thanks to you, the road is now safe for travel once more." | #177 |
| M177b | Yugiri's kinsmen fought fiercest in Doma's doomed rising. | *Why We Adventure* · 2.2 · main | "Lost but not forgotten─Lady Yugiri's kinsmen least of all. They fought fiercest, though they knew full well it was in vain." | #177 |
| M177c | The Domans vow that Revenant's Toll will prosper by their hands. | *Why We Adventure* · 2.2 · main | "Whatever work there is to be done we shall do without question. Ditch digging, bricklaying, it matters not─Revenant's Toll will grow and prosper by our hands." | #177 |
| M177d | Two Doman children resolve to found a Doman Adventurers' Guild in Revenant's Toll. | *Why We Adventure* · 2.2 · main | "the two children resolve there and then to form a [[Doman Adventurers' Guild]] once they reach [[Revenant's Toll]]." | #177 |
| M177e | Alphinaud sends you and Yugiri to Revenant's Toll. | *Why We Adventure* · 2.2 · main | "You and Lady Yugiri should travel to Revenant's Toll forthwith." | #177 |
| M178a | Slafborn welcomes Yugiri to Revenant's Toll as an equal. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "Slafborn warmly welcomes Yugiri to Revenant's Toll, insisting that they dispense with unnecessary formality and treat each other as equals." | #178 |
| M178b | The Scions still cannot reach the Students of Baldesion. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "Our attempts to reach the Students of Baldesion continue to meet with failure." | #178 |
| M178c | Y'shtola confirms in Vylbrand that the Serpent Reavers were behind the crystal thefts. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "[[Y'shtola]] returns from her investigation in [[Vylbrand]], having confirmed beyond a shadow of a doubt that [[the Serpent Reavers]] were behind the crystal thefts." | #178 |
| M178d | The Sahagin are preparing to summon Leviathan again, and Admiral Merlwyb asks the Scions' aid. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "All evidence suggests that the [[Sahagin]] are preparing to summon [[Leviathan]] once more, and Admiral [[Merlwyb]] has petitioned the [[Scions]]' aid in preventing the [[primal]]'s return." | #178 |
| M178e | Minfilia recognizes Elidibus's words in a Sahagin's vision: he is behind this. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "Even before the Sahagin made mention of the emissary, I recognized Elidibus's words. ''He'' is behind this." | #178 |
| M178f | Minfilia joins the mission to learn the true nature of the Echo. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "Desiring to learn the true nature of [[the Echo]], Minfilia declares her intent to join you on this mission." | #178 |
| M178g | Yugiri lends the Scions her blade. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "In return for the kindness you have shown my people, I would lend you my blade." | #178 |
| M178h | Thancred goes with Minfilia as her bodyguard. | *All Due Respect* · 2.2 · main | "Very well. I shall not stand in your way...on the condition that you permit me to accompany you as bodyguard." | #178 |
| M179a | If Leviathan is summoned, the only recourse is battle. | *The Sea Rises* · 2.2 · main | "Plainly, should the worst come to pass, and Leviathan is summoned, our only recourse will be to face him in battle." | #179 |
| M179b | The Sahagin lacked the crystals to summon their god until the Serpent Reavers brought more from Thanalan. | *The Sea Rises* · 2.2 · main | "According to our best intelligence, the Sahagin had not crystals enough to summon their god. … Alas, we did not count on the Serpent Reavers venturing as far as Thanalan to supplement their cache." | #179 |
| M179c | The Admiral has sent Maelstrom scouts into the Sapsa Spawning Grounds. | *The Sea Rises* · 2.2 · main | "In the command room, the Admiral informs you that [[Maelstrom]] scouts have been dispatched to reconnoiter [[Sahagin]] movement within the [[Sapsa Spawning Grounds]]." | #179 |
| M179d | The Maelstrom readies an operation to stop the summoning and wants the Scions' support. | *The Sea Rises* · 2.2 · main | "Even as we speak, the Maelstrom makes ready to launch a large-scale operation to thwart the summoning, and we would welcome the Scions' support in this endeavor." | #179 |
| M179e | Minfilia goes to the front because there is something she would see with her own eyes. | *The Sea Rises* · 2.2 · main | "I will say only that there is something I would see with mine own eyes." | #179 |
| M180a | Serpent Reavers ambushed the Maelstrom's scouts. | *Scouts in Distress* · 2.2 · main | "We were ambushed by the Serpent Reavers. Lightly armed as we are, we didn't stand a chance." | #180 |
| M180b | Two captured scouts were slain before you arrived. | *Scouts in Distress* · 2.2 · main | "Alas, you have arrived too late to save two of the scouts, who were slain in captivity." | #180 |
| M180c | At a dear price, the Maelstrom has its intelligence, and the operation may begin. | *Scouts in Distress* · 2.2 · main | "Though the price was dear, [[the Maelstrom]] now has the intelligence it sought, and the operation may commence." | #180 |
| M181a | The aim is to take the Sapsa aetheryte and kill the priest leading the summoning. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "Remember: our ultimate objective is to take the Sapsa aetheryte and eliminate the Sahagin priest presiding over the summoning─we are not here to kill them all." | #181 |
| M181b | Minfilia senses the Echo as the Sahagin priest offers himself to Leviathan. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "I know this sensation... It's the Echo!" | #181 |
| M181c | The dying priest curses the Emissary who promised him everlasting life. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "Curse you, Emissary! You promised me...everlasssting..." | #181 |
| M181d | Leviathan rises and destroys the diversionary squadron. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "Leviathan wastes no time. The diversionary squadron is lost." | #181 |
| M181e | Merlwyb fears a tidal wave that could raze coastal settlements. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "Enough to raze entire coastal settlements, if his last attempt is any guide." | #181 |
| M181f | The Sahagin have made thralls of soldiers and civilians. | *The Gift of Eternity* · 2.2 · main | "Damn them... They have made thralls of soldiers and civilians both..." | #181 |
| M182a | After the Calamity, Leviathan's tidal wave leveled Halfstone. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "he summoned a tidal wave which fair leveled Halfstone, and washed the soil away for good measure." | #182 |
| M182b | With so many stolen crystals, Leviathan is stronger than before. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "Given the quantity of crystals stolen to feed him and his legion of thralls, we can be fairly sure that Leviathan is stronger now than in his previous incarnations." | #182 |
| M182c | The Company of Heroes beat Leviathan by luring him into an inlet; now he must be fought on the open sea. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "The Company of Heroes defeated Leviathan having first lured him into an inlet, but we must needs contend with him upon the open sea." | #182 |
| M182d | A Storm Marshal's device would draw on corrupted crystals. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "They draw upon the power of corrupted crystals, I am told." | #182 |
| M182e | He proposes lashing two ships together. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "It is said that he lashed two ships together, side by side, the better to bear his plunder." | #182 |
| M182f | The twin vessel is complete. | *Into the Heart of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "At any rate, I have tidings for you: the twin vessel is now complete." | #182 |
| M183a | The Whorleater's corrupted crystals should rob Leviathan of his hold over water. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "Assuming the thing works, it will use the power of the corrupted crystals on board to rob Leviathan of his hold over water." | #183 |
| M183b | Leviathan calls you tainted of the Light. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "You trespass upon my domain, tainted of the Light!" | #183 |
| M183c | You vanquish Leviathan on the high seas. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "Following a titanic struggle on the high seas, you succeed in vanquishing Leviathan." | #183 |
| M183d | Thancred knows of a secret fraternity in Limsa Lominsa. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "Few are privy to this information, but Limsa Lominsa is home to a certain...secret fraternity." | #183 |
| M183e | Merlwyb pledges provisions to the Domans. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "Yet I'll not have it said that we turned a blind eye to your suffering. Until such time as we can do more, I pledge to send provisions." | #183 |
| M183f | A chance meeting with Alphinaud's grandsire took Thancred from Limsa Lominsa, and he trained in the blade in Ul'dah. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "But for a chance encounter with Alphinaud's grandsire, he might never have left Limsa Lominsa; … or taken up his post in Ul'dah─which is where he trained in the blade, lest you wonder." | #183 |
| M183g | Merlwyb grants Y'shtola that man bears part of the blame for the primals. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "I have not forgotten our conversation, Y'shtola. I am aware that man bears part of the blame for the primals' existence." | #183 |
| M183h | Merlwyb holds that when coexistence fails, strength decides. | *Lord of the Whorl* · 2.2 · main | "When hopes of coexistence founder, strength must determine who has the greater right to live." | #183 |
| M184a | A letter from Merlwyb gains Yugiri entry to the Lominsan underworld. | *When Yugiri Met the Fraternity* · 2.2 · main | "Zanthael gives you a [[Merlwyb's Letter of Introduction|letter of introduction from Merlwyb]], which should grant [[Yugiri]] access to the Lominsan underworld." | #184 |
| M184b | Yugiri of Doma finds kinship in the fraternity's art of stealth. | *When Yugiri Met the Fraternity* · 2.2 · main | "I am Yugiri of Doma. … 'Tis in stealth that our strength lies. There is much we might learn from one another, I think." | #184 |
| M184c | Yugiri hopes to teach her martial art to the people of Eorzea. | *When Yugiri Met the Fraternity* · 2.2 · main | "Ere long, I hope to begin imparting my martial knowledge to the people of Eorzea." | #184 |
| M184d | The fraternity's gatekeeper knows Thancred of old. | *When Yugiri Met the Fraternity* · 2.2 · main | "I was hopin' I might catch a glimpse of Thancred, too─been a while since I saw his shite-eatin' grin." | #184 |
| M185a | The slain Sahagin elder's spirit left his body, a consciousness without form. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "When the Admiral subsequently slew him, his spirit emerged from his lifeless flesh, a consciousness shorn of physical form." | #185 |
| M185b | He was then absorbed into Leviathan. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "As we both bore witness, he was ultimately absorbed into Leviathan." | #185 |
| M185c | Minfilia reasons that the Ascians, existing the same way, can be destroyed. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "If the Ascians' mode of existence is indeed the same, it can be inferred that they, too, are not invulnerable─that they ''can'' be destroyed." | #185 |
| M185d | A legend tells of souls reborn at each Umbral Calamity to stay the Darkness. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "There exists a legend which tells of souls who are reborn upon the cusp of each Umbral Calamity, that they might stay the encroaching Darkness." | #185 |
| M185e | Elidibus spoke of an existence without end. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "It was of this that Elidibus spoke─an existence which knows neither cessation nor oblivion." | #185 |
| M185f | The Isle of Val, home of the Students of Baldesion, is no more. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "My lady...the Isle of Val, which for many years hath been the order's home, is no more." | #185 |
| M185g | A magick like Ultima is thought to have been used. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "'Tis postulated that a magick was evoked, like in power to Ultima." | #185 |
| M185h | Teledji's servant does not understand the orders he is given. | *Through the Maelstrom* · 2.2 · main | "F-Forgive my impertinence, my lord, but these orders... I am uncertain as to what end they serve." | #185 |
| M186a | A great aetheric disturbance in the Twelveswood may mean a primal. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "Yet all is not well in the Twelveswood. Our latest readings reveal an ongoing aetheric disturbance of considerable magnitude. They may even suggest the presence of a primal." | #186 |
| M186b | Refugees from Ul'dah come to Revenant's Toll seeking asylum. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "refugees hailing from [[Ul'dah]] have come to [[Revenant's Toll]] in search of asylum, like the [[Doma]]ns before them." | #186 |
| M186c | Minfilia denies the refugees' request. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "And so it is with great regret that I must deny your request." | #186 |
| M186d | Alphinaud is injured in a riot begun by refugees in Ul'dah. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "that [[Alphinaud]] was injured during a riot precipitated by refugees in Ul'dah" | #186 |
| M186e | Much of the Immortal Flames is away on joint exercises. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "a large contingent of [[the Immortal Flames]] is currently engaged in joint training exercises, rendering the [[Grand Company]] undermanned at this critical juncture." | #186 |
| M186f | The riot grew from tensions between refugees and citizens after the Domans' failed bid for asylum. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "The riot, it seems, was the result of deep-seated tensions between refugees and Ul'dahn citizens, brought to the fore in the wake of the Domans' failed bid for asylum." | #186 |
| M186g | Alphinaud is not badly hurt. | *The Great Divide* · 2.3 · main | "Alphinaud's condition is not so grave as you were given to believe." | #186 |
| M187a | Alphinaud suspects the riot was not spontaneous. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "Nevertheless, I have reason to believe that this particular riot may not have begun spontaneously." | #187 |
| M187b | Raubahn secured the city, but unrest has spread through Thanalan. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "We secured the city soon enough, but not before the unrest had spread to the surrounding territories." | #187 |
| M187c | Several rioting refugees carried martial weapons. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "I observed that several of the refugees were armed─and not with butcher's knives or pitchforks, but with martial weaponry." | #187 |
| M187d | Raubahn puts restoring order before any investigation. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "That is my first duty. Until it is done, any investigation can wait." | #187 |
| M187e | A merchant recruited refugees at Lost Hope to hold Ul'dah's rulers to account. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "Apparently, he offered the refugees a chance to hold the rulers of [[Ul'dah]] to account, and attracted no shortage of volunteers." | #187 |
| M187f | He armed them and split them into two groups. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "the merchant furnished his fellows with weapons so that they might defend themselves against Ul'dahn forces, and then split them into two groups." | #187 |
| M187g | An Immortal Flames patrol cut down one group. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "Unfortunately for them, the survivor's group encountered an Immortal Flames patrol, who swiftly gave the refugees cause to rue their lack of training" | #187 |
| M187h | The other group is still out there. | *Desperate Times* · 2.3 · main | "Oh gods, the other group! They're still out there! If we don't stop them, they'll be massacred like mine was!" | #187 |
| M188a | You defeat the group's sellsword leaders before them all. | *Shock and Awe* · 2.3 · main | "challenge their leaders, twin sellswords, to combat and defeat them as all watch." | #188 |
| M188b | Their defeat ends the fight. | *Shock and Awe* · 2.3 · main | "Fortunately, their defeat seems to have had the desired effect." | #188 |
| M188c | The merchant will keep recruiting desperate refugees. | *Shock and Awe* · 2.3 · main | "the merchant will attempt to recruit more desperate refugees, perpetuating the bloodshed" | #188 |
| M188d | A shadowy figure is sowing dissent. | *Shock and Awe* · 2.3 · main | "Who is this shadowy figure intent on sowing dissent?" | #188 |
| M189a | The merchant meant to recruit in Stonesthrow. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "Recalling that the merchant wished to approach the people of [[Stonesthrow]], the survivor bids you continue your search there." | #189 |
| M189b | A girl's parents left with the merchant, who promised they could live inside the walls. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "The man said they were gonna change everything─make it so we could live inside the walls with everyone else." | #189 |
| M189c | Cornered, the merchant offers to tell all if you protect him. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "We both know I am not the one you want. However, if you agree to protect me, I swear I shall tell you everything." | #189 |
| M189d | An unseen archer kills the merchant. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "The merchant is shot and killed by an arrow from an unknown sniper." | #189 |
| M189e | He was silenced before he could talk. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "he was ''silenced''. Too many knew his face, and he was ready to divulge his secrets." | #189 |
| M189f | The true orchestrator of the riots may soon be found. | *Reap the Whirlwind* · 2.3 · main | "We may be closer to identifying the true orchestrator of these riots than you realize." | #189 |
| M190a | Raubahn summons you to a private meeting in the Fragrant Chamber. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "General Raubahn has summoned you to a private meeting in [[the Fragrant Chamber]]" | #190 |
| M190b | Brass Blades loosed arrows on unarmed protesters. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "A unit of Brass Blades sent to supervise a demonstration loosed arrows upon unarmed protesters." | #190 |
| M190c | Their commander was paid to give the order by a merchant in Teledji's employ. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "The dog confessed that a merchant had offered him coin to give the order─a merchant in the employ of Teledji Adeledji." | #190 |
| M190d | Teledji orchestrated the refugee uprising from the start. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "[[Teledji Adeledji]] is one such man, having orchestrated the refugee uprising from the start." | #190 |
| M190e | A bill would annex the Carteneau Flats for refugee settlements. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "It is a proposal to annex the Carteneau Flats so that refugees may establish permanent settlements." | #190 |
| M190f | An Allagan weapon lies in the Flats, said to have been made to fell Bahamut. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "Allagan inscriptions indicate that it was created to fell Bahamut himself." | #190 |
| M190g | Omega has long since ceased to function. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "At present, Omega is more akin to a fossil than a tool of war, having long since ceased to function." | #190 |
| M190h | Raubahn believes Teledji wants Omega's power to subjugate Ul'dah and Eorzea. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "Power enough to subjugate Ul'dah, like as not, and the rest of Eorzea besides─which is doubtless why Teledji Adeledji yearns to have it." | #190 |
| M190i | Your allies are not yet ready to move against Teledji. | *Revolution* · 2.3 · main | "Yet despite having learned of Teledji Adeledji's schemes, it would seem that your allies are not yet ready to move against him" | #190 |
| M191a | Alphinaud wonders how long the Scions would have been kept from knowing of Omega. | *Stories We Tell* · 2.3 · main | "How long do you think they intended to hide the existence of Omega from the Scions?" | #191 |
| M191b | Alphinaud is sickened that one man's lust for power caused the chaos. | *Stories We Tell* · 2.3 · main | "the thought that all this chaos was the product of one man's lust for power sickens me." | #191 |
| M191c | Alphinaud offers to help the girl search for her parents. | *Stories We Tell* · 2.3 · main | "Although he clearly has no desire to do so, Alphinaud offers to assist the girl with her search." | #191 |
| M191d | Alphinaud fears Minfilia is too ambitious. | *Stories We Tell* · 2.3 · main | "Sometimes I fear Minfilia is too ambitious." | #191 |
| M191e | Minfilia calls you back: the Twelveswood has taken a turn for the worse. | *Stories We Tell* · 2.3 · main | "The situation in the Twelveswood has taken a turn for the worse." | #191 |
| M192a | The Twin Adder believes the sylphs have summoned their guardian, the primal Ramuh. | *Lord of Levin* · 2.3 · main | "They have reason to believe that the sylphs may have called forth their revered guardian─the primal Ramuh." | #192 |
